theorem N33Affine.initial_stage_archive_local_approximation : ∀ {m : Nat} {a b t : Real} {E : (Fin m → Real) → Real}
  (hm : @LT.lt.{0} Nat instLTNat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 0) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 0))) m)
  (hat : @LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE a t) (htb : @LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE t b)
  (htbStrict : @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT t b) (Oracle : N33Affine.SliceOracle m a b t E)
  (hE :
    ∀ (r : Fin m → Real),
      @N33Affine.InCube m a b r →
        @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Real Real.instZero)) (E r))
  {Ffinal : N33Affine.BoxFamily m a b}
  (A :
    @N33Affine.StageArchive m a b t E hat htb Oracle (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))) b
      (@N33Affine.baseBoxFamily m a b t hat htb)
      (@N33Affine.exists_initial_stage_history m a b t E hm hat htb htbStrict Oracle hE) Ffinal)
  (hSeq : Nat → Real) (pSeq : Nat → Fin m → Real) (p : Fin m → Real)
  (hh :
    @Filter.Tendsto.{0, 0} Nat Real hSeq (@Filter.atTop.{0} Nat Nat.instPreorder)
      (@nhdsWithin.{0} Real
        (@UniformSpace.toTopologicalSpace.{0} Real (@PseudoMetricSpace.toUniformSpace.{0} Real Real.pseudoMetricSpace))
        (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Real Real.instZero))
        (@Set.Ioi.{0} Real Real.instPreorder
          (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Real Real.instZero)))))
  (hp :
    @Filter.Tendsto.{0, 0} Nat (Fin m → Real) pSeq (@Filter.atTop.{0} Nat Nat.instPreorder)
      (@nhds.{0} (Fin m → Real)
        (@Pi.topologicalSpace.{0, 0} (Fin m) (fun (a : Fin m) => Real) fun (i : Fin m) =>
          @UniformSpace.toTopologicalSpace.{0} Real (@PseudoMetricSpace.toUniformSpace.{0} Real Real.pseudoMetricSpace))
        p))
  (hpCube : @N33Affine.InCube m a b p) (hpMin : @N33Affine.AtMinSlice m p t)
  (j : @N33Affine.FiniteFamily.Index m (@N33Affine.BoxFamily.family m a b Ffinal))

    (E (@N33Affine.Label.slope m (@N33Affine.FiniteFamily.label m (@N33Affine.BoxFamily.family m a b Ffinal) j))) :=

theorem N33Affine.exists_local_window_of_lower_dimensions : ∀ {m : Nat} {a b t : Real} {E : (Fin m → Real) → Real}
  (hm : @LT.lt.{0} Nat instLTNat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 0) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 0))) m)
  (hat : @LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE a t) (htb : @LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE t b)
  (OracleHyp :
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      @LT.lt.{0} Nat instLTNat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 0) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 0))) k →
        @LT.lt.{0} Nat instLTNat k m →
          ∀ (β : Real),
            @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT β b →
              ∀ (E₀ : (Fin k → Real) → Real),
                (∀ (s : Fin k → Real),
                    @N33Affine.InCube k β b s →
                      @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT
                        (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Real Real.instZero)) (E₀ s)) →
                  @Exists.{2} (N33Affine.BoxFamily k β b) fun (F : N33Affine.BoxFamily k β b) =>
                    And (@N33Affine.FamilyLittleOAt k β b F β)
                      (@N33Affine.FullApproximation k β b E₀ (@N33Affine.BoxFamily.family k β b F)))
  (hE :
    ∀ (r : Fin m → Real),
      @N33Affine.InCube m a b r →
        @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Real Real.instZero)) (E r)),
  Nonempty.{2} (@N33Affine.LocalWindowData m a b E t) :=
fun {m : ⋯} {a b t : ⋯} {E : ⋯} (hm : ⋯) (hat : ⋯) (htb : ⋯) (OracleHyp : ⋯) (hE : ⋯) =>
  @dite.{0} (Nonempty.{2} (@N33Affine.LocalWindowData m a b E t)) (@Eq.{1} Real t b) (Real.decidableEq t b)
    (fun (htbEq : ⋯) =>
      @Eq.ndrec.{0, 1} Real t
        (fun {b : Real} =>
          ∀ (htb : @LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE t b)
            (OracleHyp :
              ∀ (k : Nat),
                @LT.lt.{0} Nat instLTNat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 0) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 0))) k →
                  @LT.lt.{0} Nat instLTNat k m →
                    ∀ (β : Real),
                      @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT β b →
                        ∀ (E₀ : (Fin k → Real) → Real),
                          (∀ (s : Fin k → Real),
                              @N33Affine.InCube k β b s →
                                @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT
                                  (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Real Real.instZero)) (E₀ s)) →
                            @Exists.{2} (N33Affine.BoxFamily k β b) fun (F : N33Affine.BoxFamily k β b) =>
                              And (@N33Affine.FamilyLittleOAt k β b F β)
                                (@N33Affine.FullApproximation k β b E₀ (@N33Affine.BoxFamily.family k β b F)))
            (hE :
              ∀ (r : Fin m → Real),
                @N33Affine.InCube m a b r →
                  @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT
                    (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Real Real.instZero)) (E r)),
            Nonempty.{2} (@N33Affine.LocalWindowData m a b E t))
        (fun (htb : ⋯) (OracleHyp : ⋯) (hE : ⋯) =>
          let F : ⋯ := @N33Affine.baseBoxFamily m a t t hat (@le_rfl.{0} Real Real.instPreorder t);
          let slope0 : ⋯ := fun (x : Fin m) => t;
          have hE0 : ⋯ :=
            hE slope0 fun (i : Fin m) =>
              @And.intro (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE a (slope0 i)) (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE (slope0 i) t) hat

theorem N33Affine.Glue.active_label_inherits_local_guarantee.{u_1} : ∀ {N m : Nat} {ι : Fin N → Type u_1} (a b : Real)
  (E : (Fin m → Real) → Real) (labels : (j : Fin N) → ι j → N33Affine.Label m) (q : Nat → Real) (lo hi : Fin N → Real)
  (hLocal : @N33Affine.Glue.LocalNeighborhoodGuarantee.{u_1} N m ι a b E labels lo hi) (j : Fin N) (k : ι j)
  (hSeq : Nat → Real) (pSeq : Nat → Fin m → Real) (p : Fin m → Real) (s : Real)
  (hSeqT :
    @Filter.Tendsto.{0, 0} Nat Real hSeq (@Filter.atTop.{0} Nat Nat.instPreorder)
      (@nhdsWithin.{0} Real
        (@UniformSpace.toTopologicalSpace.{0} Real (@PseudoMetricSpace.toUniformSpace.{0} Real Real.pseudoMetricSpace))
        (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Real Real.instZero))
        (@Set.Ioi.{0} Real Real.instPreorder
          (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Real Real.instZero)))))
  (hpT :
    @Filter.Tendsto.{0, 0} Nat (Fin m → Real) pSeq (@Filter.atTop.{0} Nat Nat.instPreorder)
      (@nhds.{0} (Fin m → Real)
        (@Pi.topologicalSpace.{0, 0} (Fin m) (fun (a : Fin m) => Real) fun (i : Fin m) =>
          @UniformSpace.toTopologicalSpace.{0} Real (@PseudoMetricSpace.toUniformSpace.{0} Real Real.pseudoMetricSpace))
        p))
  (hpCube : @N33Affine.InCube m a b p) (hslice : @N33Affine.AtMinSlice m p s)
  (hslabLower : @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT (lo j) s) (hslabUpper : @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT s (hi j))
  (hactive :
    @Filter.Eventually.{0} Nat
      (fun (n : Nat) =>
        @N33Affine.Active.{u_1} m (@Sigma.{0, u_1} (Fin N) fun (j : Fin N) => ι j)
          (@N33Affine.Glue.taggedLabels.{u_1} N m ι labels q) (@Sigma.mk.{0, u_1} (Fin N) (fun (j : Fin N) => ι j) j k)
          (hSeq n) (pSeq n))
      (@Filter.atTop.{0} Nat Nat.instPreorder))
  (i : Fin m),
  @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT
    (@abs.{0} Real Real.lattice Real.instAddGroup
      (@HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} Real Real Real (@instHSub.{0} Real Real.instSub) (p i)
        (@N33Affine.Label.slope m (labels j k) i)))
    (E (@N33Affine.Label.slope m (labels j k))) :=
fun {N m : Nat} {ι : Fin N → Type u_1} (a b : Real) (E : (Fin m → Real) → Real)
    (labels : (j : Fin N) → ι j → N33Affine.Label m) (q : Nat → Real) (lo hi : Fin N → Real)
    (hLocal : @N33Affine.Glue.LocalNeighborhoodGuarantee.{u_1} N m ι a b E labels lo hi) (j : Fin N) (k : ι j)
    (hSeq : Nat → Real) (pSeq : Nat → Fin m → Real) (p : Fin m → Real) (s : Real)

            (@PseudoMetricSpace.toUniformSpace.{0} Real Real.pseudoMetricSpace))

    (hpT :
      @Filter.Tendsto.{0, 0} Nat (Fin m → Real) pSeq (@Filter.atTop.{0} Nat Nat.instPreorder)
        (@nhds.{0} (Fin m → Real)
          (@Pi.topologicalSpace.{0, 0} (Fin m) (fun (a : Fin m) => Real) fun (i : Fin m) =>
            @UniformSpace.toTopologicalSpace.{0} Real
              (@PseudoMetricSpace.toUniformSpace.{0} Real Real.pseudoMetricSpace))
          p))
    (hpCube : @N33Affine.InCube m a b p) (hslice : @N33Affine.AtMinSlice m p s)
    (hslabLower : @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT (lo j) s) (hslabUpper : @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT s (hi j))
    (hactive :
      @Filter.Eventually.{0} Nat
        (fun (n : Nat) =>
          @N33Affine.Active.{u_1} m (@Sigma.{0, u_1} (Fin N) fun (j : Fin N) => ι j)

            (@Sigma.mk.{0, u_1} (Fin N) (fun (j : Fin N) => ι j) j k) (hSeq n) (pSeq n))
        (@Filter.atTop.{0} Nat Nat.instPreorder)) =>
  hLocal j k hSeq pSeq p s hSeqT hpT hpCube hslice hslabLower hslabUpper
    (@Filter.mp_mem.{0} Nat (@Filter.atTop.{0} Nat Nat.instPreorder)
      (@setOf.{0} Nat fun (x : Nat) =>
        (fun (n : Nat) =>
            @N33Affine.Active.{u_1} m (@Sigma.{0, u_1} (Fin N) fun (j : Fin N) => ι j)
              (@N33Affine.Glue.taggedLabels.{u_1} N m ι labels q)
              (@Sigma.mk.{0, u_1} (Fin N) (fun (j : Fin N) => ι j) j k) (hSeq n) (pSeq n))

      (@Filter.univ_mem'.{0} Nat (@Filter.atTop.{0} Nat Nat.instPreorder)
        (@setOf.{0} Nat fun (x : Nat) =>

              (@setOf.{0} Nat fun (x : Nat) =>
                (fun (n : Nat) =>

                  x)
              x →
            @Membership.mem.{0, 0} Nat (Set.{0} Nat) (@Set.instMembership.{0} Nat)

            (hn :

                (@Sigma.mk.{0, u_1} (Fin N) (fun (j : Fin N) => ι j) j k) (hSeq n) (pSeq n)) =>
          @N33Affine.Glue.active_restricts_to_local.{u_1} N m ι labels q j k (hSeq n) (pSeq n) hn)))
theorem N33Affine.GluePlacement.active_minimum_in_slab : ∀ {N m : Nat} (a b : Real)
  (hN : @LT.lt.{0} Nat instLTNat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 0) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 0))) N)
  (centers gamma : Fin N → Real) (families : Fin N → N33Affine.FiniteFamily m) (q : Nat → Real)
  (hcenterBox :
    ∀ (j : Fin N), And (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE a (centers j)) (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE (centers j) b))
  (hcenterGamma : ∀ (j : Fin N), @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT (centers j) (gamma j))
  (hcuts :
    ∀ (k : Nat)
      (hk :
        @LT.lt.{0} Nat instLTNat
          (@HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHAdd.{0} Nat instAddNat) k
            (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))))
          N),
      And
        (@LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT
          (centers
            (@Fin.mk N k
              (@Decidable.byContradiction (@LT.lt.{0} Nat instLTNat k N) (Nat.decLt k N)
                fun (a : Not (@LT.lt.{0} Nat instLTNat k N)) =>
                @N33Affine.GluePlacement.active_minimum_in_slab._proof_1 N k hk a)))
          (q k))
        (And
          (@LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT (q k)
            (centers
              (@Fin.mk N
                (@HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHAdd.{0} Nat instAddNat) k
                  (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))))
                hk)))
          (@LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT (q k)
            (gamma
              (@Fin.mk N k
                (@Decidable.byContradiction (@LT.lt.{0} Nat instLTNat k N) (Nat.decLt k N)
                  fun (a : Not (@LT.lt.{0} Nat instLTNat k N)) =>
                  @N33Affine.GluePlacement.active_minimum_in_slab._proof_1 N k hk a))))))
  (hfloor :
    ∀ (j : Fin N) (k : @N33Affine.FiniteFamily.Index m (families j)) (i : Fin m),
      @LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE (centers j)
        (@N33Affine.Label.slope m (@N33Affine.FiniteFamily.label m (families j) k) i))
  (hbumps :
    ∀ (j : Fin N) (k : @N33Affine.FiniteFamily.Index m (families j)),
      N33Affine.LittleO (@N33Affine.Label.bump m (@N33Affine.FiniteFamily.label m (families j) k)) (gamma j))
  (hbase :
    ∀ (j : Fin N),
      @Exists.{1} (@N33Affine.FiniteFamily.Index m (families j))
        fun (k : @N33Affine.FiniteFamily.Index m (families j)) =>
        @Eq.{1} (N33Affine.Label m) (@N33Affine.FiniteFamily.label m (families j) k)
          (@N33Affine.baselineLabel m (centers j)))
  (j : Fin N) (k : @N33Affine.FiniteFamily.Index m (families j)) (hSeq : Nat → Real) (pSeq : Nat → Fin m → Real)
  (p : Fin m → Real) (s : Real)
  (hh :
    @Filter.Tendsto.{0, 0} Nat Real hSeq (@Filter.atTop.{0} Nat Nat.instPreorder)
      (@nhdsWithin.{0} Real
        (@UniformSpace.toTopologicalSpace.{0} Real (@PseudoMetricSpace.toUniformSpace.{0} Real Real.pseudoMetricSpace))
        (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Real Real.instZero))
        (@Set.Ioi.{0} Real Real.instPreorder
          (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Real Real.instZero)))))
  (hp :
    @Filter.Tendsto.{0, 0} Nat (Fin m → Real) pSeq (@Filter.atTop.{0} Nat Nat.instPreorder)
      (@nhds.{0} (Fin m → Real)
        (@Pi.topologicalSpace.{0, 0} (Fin m) (fun (a : Fin m) => Real) fun (i : Fin m) =>
          @UniformSpace.toTopologicalSpace.{0} Real (@PseudoMetricSpace.toUniformSpace.{0} Real Real.pseudoMetricSpace))
        p))
  (hpCube : @N33Affine.InCube m a b p) (hslice : @N33Affine.AtMinSlice m p s)
  (hactive :
    @Filter.Eventually.{0} Nat
      (fun (n : Nat) =>
        @N33Affine.Active.{0} m
          (@N33Affine.FiniteFamily.Index m (@N33Affine.GluePlacement.shiftedUnion N m families q hN))
          (@N33Affine.FiniteFamily.label m (@N33Affine.GluePlacement.shiftedUnion N m families q hN))
          (@Sigma.mk.{0, 0} (Fin N) (fun (j : Fin N) => @N33Affine.FiniteFamily.Index m (families j)) j k) (hSeq n)
          (pSeq n))
      (@Filter.atTop.{0} Nat Nat.instPreorder)),
  And (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE (@N33Affine.GluePlacement.slabLower N a q j) s)
    (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE s (@N33Affine.GluePlacement.slabUpper N b q j)) :=
fun {N m : ⋯} (a b : ⋯) (hN : ⋯) (centers gamma : ⋯) (families : ⋯) (q : ⋯) (hcenterBox : ⋯) (hcenterGamma : ⋯)

    (hp : ⋯) (hpCube : ⋯) (hslice : ⋯) (hactive : ⋯) =>
  have has : ⋯ :=
    @Exists.casesOn.{1} (Fin m) (fun (i : Fin m) => @Eq.{1} Real (p i) s)
      (fun (x : @Exists.{1} (Fin m) fun (i : Fin m) => @Eq.{1} Real (p i) s) => @LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE a s)
      (@And.right (∀ (i : Fin m), @LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE s (p i))
        (@Exists.{1} (Fin m) fun (i : Fin m) => @Eq.{1} Real (p i) s) hslice)

      have this : @LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE a (p i) :=
        @And.left (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE a (p i)) (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE (p i) b) (hpCube i);
      @Trans.trans.{0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1} Real Real Real (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE) (@Eq.{1} Real)

        hi;
  have hsb : ⋯ :=
    @Exists.casesOn.{1} (Fin m) (fun (i : Fin m) => @Eq.{1} Real (p i) s)

      (@And.right (∀ (i : Fin m), @LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE s (p i))
        (@Exists.{1} (Fin m) fun (i : Fin m) => @Eq.{1} Real (p i) s) hslice)

      have this : @LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE (p i) b :=
        @And.right (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE a (p i)) (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE (p i) b) (hpCube i);

        (@Eq.symm.{1} Real (p i) s hi) this;
  @Exists.casesOn.{1} (@N33Affine.FiniteFamily.Index m (families j))
    (fun (k : @N33Affine.FiniteFamily.Index m (families j)) =>
      @Eq.{1} (N33Affine.Label m) (@N33Affine.FiniteFamily.label m (families j) k)
        (@N33Affine.baselineLabel m (centers j)))
    (fun
        (x :
          @Exists.{1} (@N33Affine.FiniteFamily.Index m (families j))
            fun (k : @N33Affine.FiniteFamily.Index m (families j)) =>
            @Eq.{1} (N33Affine.Label m) (@N33Affine.FiniteFamily.label m (families j) k)
              (@N33Affine.baselineLabel m (centers j))) =>
      And (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE (@N33Affine.GluePlacement.slabLower N a q j) s)
        (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE s (@N33Affine.GluePlacement.slabUpper N b q j)))
    (hbase j) fun (kb : ⋯) (hbaseEq : ⋯) =>
    have hlo : ⋯ :=
      @dite.{0} (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE (@N33Affine.GluePlacement.slabLower N a q j) s)
        (@Eq.{1} Nat (@Fin.val N j) (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 0) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 0))))
        (instDecidableEqNat (@Fin.val N j) (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 0) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 0))))
        (fun (hj : @Eq.{1} Nat (@Fin.val N j) (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 0) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 0)))) =>

              (@Eq.{1} Prop (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE (@N33Affine.GluePlacement.slabLower N a q j) s)

                (@congrArg.{1, 1} Real (Real → Prop) (@N33Affine.GluePlacement.slabLower N a q j) a

                      True
                      (@congrFun'.{1, 1} Nat Prop (@Eq.{1} Nat (@Fin.val N j))

                        (@congrArg.{1, 1} Nat (Nat → Prop) (@Fin.val N j)
                          (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 0) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 0))) (@Eq.{1} Nat) hj)

                      (@eq_self.{1} Nat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 0) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 0)))))))

        let jp : ⋯ :=
          @Fin.mk N
            (@HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHSub.{0} Nat instSubNat) (@Fin.val N j)

N33Affine.SliceInput.approx {k : Nat} {β b : Real} {E : (Fin k → Real) → Real} (self : N33Affine.SliceInput k β b E)
  (j : @N33Affine.FiniteFamily.Index k (@N33Affine.SliceInput.family k β b E self)) (hSeq : Nat → Real)
  (pSeq : Nat → Fin k → Real) (p : Fin k → Real) :
  @Filter.Tendsto.{0, 0} Nat Real hSeq (@Filter.atTop.{0} Nat Nat.instPreorder)
      (@nhdsWithin.{0} Real
        (@UniformSpace.toTopologicalSpace.{0} Real (@PseudoMetricSpace.toUniformSpace.{0} Real Real.pseudoMetricSpace))
        (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Real Real.instZero))
        (@Set.Ioi.{0} Real Real.instPreorder
          (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Real Real.instZero)))) →
    @Filter.Tendsto.{0, 0} Nat (Fin k → Real) pSeq (@Filter.atTop.{0} Nat Nat.instPreorder)
        (@nhds.{0} (Fin k → Real)
          (@Pi.topologicalSpace.{0, 0} (Fin k) (fun (a : Fin k) => Real) fun (i : Fin k) =>
            @UniformSpace.toTopologicalSpace.{0} Real
              (@PseudoMetricSpace.toUniformSpace.{0} Real Real.pseudoMetricSpace))
          p) →
      @N33Affine.InCube k β b p →
        @Filter.Eventually.{0} Nat
            (fun (n : Nat) =>
              @N33Affine.Active.{0} k (@N33Affine.FiniteFamily.Index k (@N33Affine.SliceInput.family k β b E self))
                (@N33Affine.FiniteFamily.label k (@N33Affine.SliceInput.family k β b E self)) j (hSeq n) (pSeq n))
            (@Filter.atTop.{0} Nat Nat.instPreorder) →
          ∀ (i : Fin k),
            @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT
              (@abs.{0} Real Real.lattice Real.instAddGroup
                (@HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} Real Real Real (@instHSub.{0} Real Real.instSub) (p i)
                  (@N33Affine.Label.slope k
                    (@N33Affine.FiniteFamily.label k (@N33Affine.SliceInput.family k β b E self) j) i)))
              (E
                (@N33Affine.Label.slope k
                  (@N33Affine.FiniteFamily.label k (@N33Affine.SliceInput.family k β b E self) j)))
N33Affine.exists_complete_stage_family_with_certificate {m : Nat} {a b t : Real} {E : (Fin m → Real) → Real}
  (hm : @LT.lt.{0} Nat instLTNat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 0) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 0))) m)
  (hat : @LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE a t) (htb : @LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE t b)
  (htb_strict : @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT t b) (Oracle : N33Affine.SliceOracle m a b t E)
  (hE :
    ∀ (r : Fin m → Real),
      @N33Affine.InCube m a b r →
        @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Real Real.instZero)) (E r)) :
  @Exists.{1} Real fun (upperFinal : Real) =>
    @Exists.{2} (N33Affine.BoxFamily m a b) fun (F : N33Affine.BoxFamily m a b) =>
      @Exists.{1} Real fun (γ : Real) =>
        And (N33Affine.StageHistory m a b t E hat htb Oracle m upperFinal F)
          (And (@N33Affine.FamilySlopesGe m a b F t)
            (And (@N33Affine.FamilyHasFlatBaseline m a b t F)
              (And (@LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT t γ)
                (And (@N33Affine.FamilyLittleOAt m a b F γ)
                  (Nonempty.{3}
                    (@N33Affine.StageArchive m a b t E hat htb Oracle
                      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))) b
                      (@N33Affine.baseBoxFamily m a b t hat htb)
                      (@N33Affine.exists_initial_stage_history m a b t E hm hat htb htb_strict Oracle hE) F))))))
N33Affine.initial_stage_archive_local_approximation {m : Nat} {a b t : Real} {E : (Fin m → Real) → Real}
  (hm : @LT.lt.{0} Nat instLTNat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 0) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 0))) m)
  (hat : @LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE a t) (htb : @LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE t b)
  (htbStrict : @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT t b) (Oracle : N33Affine.SliceOracle m a b t E)
  (hE :
    ∀ (r : Fin m → Real),
      @N33Affine.InCube m a b r →
        @LT.lt.{0} Real Real.instLT (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Real Real.instZero)) (E r))
  {Ffinal : N33Affine.BoxFamily m a b}
  (A :
